### 2026-09-18 10:07:34  #其他

【住房城鄉建設部：住建事業“十五五”時期面臨新形勢】今天，國新辦舉行“開局起步‘十五五’”系列主題新聞釋出會，住房城鄉建設部有關負責人介紹，住建事業“十五五”時期面臨新形勢，住建事業主要有城鄉建設、住房和房地產、建築業三大板塊。三大板塊都處在轉型發展的關鍵期，概括起來是“兩個轉向、兩個轉變、三化轉型”。“兩個轉向”講的是城鄉建設板塊，是指城鎮化正從快速增長期轉向穩定發展期，城市發展正從大規模增量擴張階段轉向存量提質增效為主的階段；“兩個轉變”講的是住房和房地產板塊，一個是房地產市場供求關係發生重大變化，一個是房地產進入存量時代；“三化轉型”講的是建築業板塊，也就是建築業向智慧化、綠色化、融合化轉型。（央視新聞）

### 2026-09-18 10:06:13  #公司

【中國三峽建工（集團）有限公司原一級諮詢陳文夫被"雙開"】中央紀委國家監委網站9月18日通報，據中央紀委國家監委駐中國三峽集團紀檢監察組、湖北省紀委監委訊息：日前，經中央紀委國家監委批准，中央紀委國家監委駐中國三峽集團紀檢監察組、湖北省宜昌市監委對中國三峽建工（集團）有限公司原一級諮詢陳文夫嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，陳文夫作為黨員領導幹部，喪失理想信念，背棄初心使命，對黨不忠誠不老實，在組織談話時不如實說清問題，妄圖透過社會騙子規避組織調查，與他人串供，處心積慮對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，收受可能影響公正執行公務的禮品、禮金，接受可能影響公正執行公務的宴請和娛樂活動安排；違反組織原則，在職工錄用中為他人謀取利益；廉潔底線失守，向管理服務物件借用錢款，參與民間借貸獲取大額回報，將應由特定關係人個人支付的費用安排管理服務物件支付；追求低階趣味；貪慾膨脹，將權力異化為謀取私利的工具，利用職務便利為他人在工程專案承攬、結算等方面謀取利益，並非法收受鉅額財物。陳文夫嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律、生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經中國三峽集團黨組研究，決定給予陳文夫開除黨籍處分；經中央紀委國家監委駐中國三峽集團紀檢監察組研究，決定給予陳文夫開除公職處分；追繳其違紀違法所得。湖北省宜昌市監委將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-09-18 10:02:05  #其他

【12306回應國慶假期高鐵漲價】9月16日，國慶假期前一天高鐵票開售。有網友稱“準備搶國慶期間的車票，發現車票居然漲價了。”該網友曬出的截圖顯示，9月25日，重慶北至南京南D988次列車票價為446元起，9月30日則為520元起。另有網友分享了廣州至潮汕G6592次列車票價變化，該網友稱，“同個班次，平時價格是279元，中秋漲到298元，國慶漲到302元。”9月18日早間，12306官方客服表示，高鐵票價可能因節假日客流等因素浮動，不同日期優惠折扣不同，並非漲價。具體票價以12306官方顯示為準。對於何時購票更優惠，客服則表示，無法確保某一時段的優惠力度大小。一般情況下，開票期間不會出現較大變化，但也不排除特殊情況。（中新經緯）

### 2026-09-18 10:00:24  #宏觀

【市場監管總局釋出首個鈣鈦礦光伏領域國家標準】市場監管總局釋出首個鈣鈦礦光伏領域國家標準《光伏器件 第12部分：鈣鈦礦光伏電池及元件的電流-電壓（I-V）特性測量方法》，將於2027年正式實施。在“雙碳”目標推動下，鈣鈦礦光伏技術發展迅速，正從實驗室走向吉瓦級大規模量產，電池及元件的測試認證需求不斷增長。但鈣鈦礦器件在光照和電壓變化下效能容易波動，測試結果不夠穩定，面積定義也尚未統一，過去常用於晶矽和薄膜電池的測試方法難以直接套用。簡單來說，該標準要解決的是鈣鈦礦電池和元件到底該怎麼測才能測得準、比得公平。鈣鈦礦器件在光照下效能會變化，不能像傳統晶矽那樣快速一測即可。該標準系統規定了測試裝置、測試前的穩定化處理要求，以及如何測量電流-電壓特性、如何計算面積和發電效率等。針對實驗室研發和規模化生產的不同需要，該標準給出快速測量法，並補充最大功率點追蹤法、十點準穩態擬合法（仲裁法）、漸進法等更貼近真實工作狀態的準穩態測試方法。同時，該標準統一總面積與光闌面積定義及效率計算，讓不同機構測出的結果可以放在同一把標尺上比較，最終形成一套可驗證、可復現的標準化測試流程。該標準構建了科學、可靠、可推廣的鈣鈦礦光伏I-V特性測量方法體系，填補了我國在新興光伏技術標準化測試領域的空白。它將成為檢測認證、產品研發、產線質控和貿易交付的“統一標尺”，為後續標準體系完善、國際標準制定及鈣鈦礦光伏產業化推廣應用提供關鍵技術支撐。

### 2026-09-18 09:58:43  #MKT News #Macro & Rates #XAUUSD #Impact bullish #WTI

【RBA: Inflation risks skewed to the upside; inflation expectations may be shifting】Reserve Bank of Australia Governor Bullock told a parliamentary committee firms are increasingly forming a view that high inflation may not fall back below 3%. She said businesses are asking whether inflation will decline, which is a concern. Bullock reiterated the RBA’s dual mandate of full employment and a 2%-3% inflation target and warned the longer inflation stays above target, the greater the risk that inflation expectations begin to adjust upward. She said inflation risks are tilted to the upside, driven partly by concerns over expectations and by incoming supply shocks — including from the Middle East and weather-related events — alongside demand-side pressures.

### 2026-09-18 09:54:05  #其他

【加強涉企侵權資訊管理 中央網信辦要求平臺主動清理五大類】中央網信辦釋出《關於加強涉企侵權資訊管理 持續推動營商網路環境最佳化的通知》，通知明確處置範圍，內容如下：處置涉企侵權資訊，事關企業、企業家切身利益，事關人民群眾切身權益。經過一段時間實踐，各方對處置涉企侵權資訊已形成共識。網站平臺對以下涉企侵權資訊，應當積極主動清理處置。1.個人資訊類。包括洩露企業家身份證、護照、戶籍檔案、家庭住址、電話號碼等個人身份及聯絡資訊，以及生物識別、醫療健康、金融賬戶、行蹤軌跡等敏感個人資訊。2.侮辱貶損類。包括侮辱謾罵企業、企業家，惡搞醜化企業標識、產品和企業家肖像，將企業、企業家與色情低俗話題惡意關聯，使用汙名化詞彙詆譭企業、企業家或特定品牌使用者。3.假冒仿冒類。包括假冒仿冒新聞單位制作釋出的涉企監督資訊，假借企業或企業家名義釋出的資訊，在公眾賬號名稱、頭像、簡介中，擅自使用與企業相同或相似的名稱標識或企業家姓名肖像。4.虛假誤導類。包括斷章取義、歪曲解讀企業家過往言論，呈現與事實不符的搜尋關鍵詞聯想、搜尋落地頁結果，已經權威闢謠、澄清的不實資訊，與企業、企業家在超大平臺上的官方賬號釋出的公開宣告不符資訊。5.其他類。包括涉企拉踩引戰資訊，集納翻炒的企業舊聞舊事、負面資訊，已被本平臺處置過的涉企侵權資訊，機器人賬號、網路水軍賬號釋出的涉企評論資訊，明顯違背公序良俗抹黑詆譭企業、企業家資訊。網站平臺應當根據本通知要求，健全資訊內容管理制度，完善最佳化平臺社群規則，將涉企資訊納入日常內容稽核處置範圍，強化賬號、MCN機構管理，切實增強工作主動性、針對性和實效性。網信部門要落實屬地管理責任，指導所在地網站平臺完善制度，加強涉企資訊內容管理，及時清理涉企侵權資訊。對重視程度不足、落實力度不夠特別是網民反映問題集中的網站平臺，依法依規從嚴處理。

### 2026-09-18 09:51:47  #Trading Economics #Economy #Australia #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Australia 10Y Yield Retreats from 2011 Highs — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield fell below 5.3%, pulling back from mid-2011 highs and tracking lower global yields as easing concerns over Middle East supply disruptions reduced inflation pressures. Oil prices retreated after Saudi Arabia worked to restore flows through its East-West pipeline, while President Trump is set to meet Gulf leaders next week. In Australia, hawkish comments from the Reserve Bank’s top official reinforced expectations for further policy tightening. Governor Michele Bullock said upside inflation risks were materializing and warned that higher costs could make inflation more persistent. Her remarks follow statements over the past month by senior RBA officials that inflation remains elevated and needs to ease further. Markets now priced at least two more rate hikes by the February meeting, with a roughly 87% chance the first occurs on September 29. That marks a notable shift from the start of September, when traders were pricing just one further hike.

Gasoline Prices Ease — US gasoline futures fell to around $3.46 per gallon, slightly easing from an eight-week high, as expectations that Saudi Arabia could restore some disrupted exports soothed immediate supply concerns. The kingdom was reportedly seeking to restore about half the capacity of its East-West oil pipeline within days after drone attacks halted the key link to the Red Sea, while offering additional crude cargoes to Asian refiners via ship-to-ship transfers off Oman’s Sohar port. Nevertheless, risks remained as Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi forces exchanged fresh strikes. Meanwhile, EIA data showed US gasoline inventories unexpectedly rose for a second consecutive week, increasing by 794,000 barrels in the week ended September 11, while refinery utilization remained elevated at 96.8%, despite easing from 97.8% a week earlier. However, approaching seasonal refinery maintenance could constrain production and remains a threat to refined-product supplies.

US Heating Oil Prices Ease — US heating oil prices eased to around $5.05 per gallon, retreating from a record high of $5.26 reached earlier this week, as rising inventories and expectations of alternative routes for Middle Eastern barrels offered some respite from tight distillate supplies. EIA data showed distillate inventories, including diesel and heating oil, rose by 1.6 million barrels in the week ended September 11 but remained 13% below their five-year average. Meanwhile, Saudi Arabia was reportedly seeking to restore about half the capacity of its East-West oil pipeline within days after drone attacks halted the key link to the Red Sea, while offering additional crude cargoes to Asian refiners via ship-to-ship transfers off Oman’s Sohar port. Still, supply risks persisted as Saudi Arabia and Iran-backed Houthi forces exchanged fresh strikes. With winter approaching, distillate demand could strengthen, while refinery maintenance could constrain production and put further pressure on already-tight supplies.

### 2026-09-18 09:37:20  #MKT News #Important #Commodities #Energy #Market Regions #Greater China

【NDRC, NEA and national mine safety regulator order measures to stabilize coal production and supply】China's NDRC, NEA and the National Mine Safety Administration issued a joint notice directing coal-producing provinces and coal firms to, while ensuring safety, accelerate measures to stabilize production and secure supply. The notice mandates stronger monitoring and dispatch, optimized production planning, expedited mine resumptions and full-capacity restarts, and faster acceptance of jointly trial-run mines to support a steady rebound in coal output. It also requires tighter signing and performance of medium- and long-term thermal-coal contracts, use of imports to supplement domestic supply, and advancement of follow-up coal capacity construction. Authorities said they will enhance coordination, guide implementation, adjust policies and, where appropriate, timely release coal-capacity reserves to stabilize production and market functioning.

### 2026-09-18 09:32:27  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Stock Market #Importance 1

Australia Shares Inch Higher, Weekly Picture Muted — Australian stocks edged up 14 points, or 0.2%, to 8,747 in early Friday trade, extending gains for a third straight session after Wall Street rebounded overnight as easing oil prices and softer Treasury yields helped calm nerves after the Fed’s rate-hike shock. Strength came from tech, non-energy minerals, and consumer durables, though losses in healthcare, energy minerals, and retail trade capped the upside. Gold miners advanced, with Northern Star Resources up 2.7% and Evolution Mining rising 3.0%, while Lynas Rare Earths, Perseus Mining, and South32 each added over 2%. For the week, the market was little changed, stabilizing after recent declines as traders weighed remarks from RBA Governor Michele Bullock, who warned that upside inflation risks are emerging amid surging global energy costs from the prolonged Middle East conflict, compounded by pressures from the AI data-centre boom and extreme weather.

【Liquidity indicators: market rates continue to rebound】Early on Sept. 18 DR007 rebounded to 1.4550%, trading around the PBOC 7‑day policy rate near 1.4%. The PBOC injected 162 billion yuan via 7‑day reverse repos and, for the first time in nearly a year, conducted a 14‑day reverse repo of 100 billion yuan. DR007 is a short‑term funding benchmark; its spread versus the PBOC 7‑day repo is used to gauge funding tightness—DR007 above the policy rate signals tighter market funding, below signals looser conditions.

【加拿大對進口罐裝蔬菜產品作出保障措施終裁】近日，WTO保障措施委員會發布加拿大代表團向其提交的保障措施通報。加拿大國際貿易法庭（CITT）於2026年9月9日對進口罐頭蔬菜產品（Canned Vegetable Goods）作出保障措施肯定性終裁，認定進口增加是造成國內產業嚴重損害的主要原因，建議以關稅配額形式實施保障措施。具體如下：第1年配額為13,000,000公斤，超配額附加稅為50%；第2年配額為13,260,000公斤，超配額附加稅為45%；第3年配額為13,525,200公斤，超配額附加稅為40%。措施建議實施3年，至2030年1月5日。

【中央網信辦：加強涉企侵權資訊管理 持續推動營商網路環境最佳化】中央網信辦釋出《關於加強涉企侵權資訊管理 持續推動營商網路環境最佳化的通知》，通知指出，處置涉企侵權資訊，事關企業、企業家切身利益，事關人民群眾切身權益。經過一段時間實踐，各方對處置涉企侵權資訊已形成共識。網站平臺對以下涉企侵權資訊，應當積極主動清理處置。1.個人資訊類。包括洩露企業家身份證、護照、戶籍檔案、家庭住址、電話號碼等個人身份及聯絡資訊，以及生物識別、醫療健康、金融賬戶、行蹤軌跡等敏感個人資訊。2.侮辱貶損類。包括侮辱謾罵企業、企業家，惡搞醜化企業標識、產品和企業家肖像，將企業、企業家與色情低俗話題惡意關聯，使用汙名化詞彙詆譭企業、企業家或特定品牌使用者。3.假冒仿冒類。包括假冒仿冒新聞單位制作釋出的涉企監督資訊，假借企業或企業家名義釋出的資訊，在公眾賬號名稱、頭像、簡介中，擅自使用與企業相同或相似的名稱標識或企業家姓名肖像。4.虛假誤導類。包括斷章取義、歪曲解讀企業家過往言論，呈現與事實不符的搜尋關鍵詞聯想、搜尋落地頁結果，已經權威闢謠、澄清的不實資訊，與企業、企業家在超大平臺上的官方賬號釋出的公開宣告不符資訊。5.其他類。包括涉企拉踩引戰資訊，集納翻炒的企業舊聞舊事、負面資訊，已被本平臺處置過的涉企侵權資訊，機器人賬號、網路水軍賬號釋出的涉企評論資訊，明顯違背公序良俗抹黑詆譭企業、企業家資訊。

### 2026-09-18 09:06:35  #MKT News #Important #Market Regions #Japan & APAC #USDJPY #Impact bullish

【BOJ decision preview: 25bp hike expected; yen vulnerable if guidance isn’t hawkish】Most firms expect the BOJ to raise its policy rate by 25bp to 1.25% today; markets are focussing on forward guidance — a less hawkish signal would likely trigger yen weakness and pressure JGBs. Reuters survey: economists expect the policy rate to reach 1.50% by end-March next year and 1.75% by Q2 2027, with most seeing at least that terminal rate. Nikko expects 25bp to 1.25% and 1.75% by June 2027, with upside to 2.25% if oil rises and other central banks tighten. BNP sees 25bp now, then further hikes in Dec and next March to 1.75%. OCBC says the hike is largely priced; the key is the post-September path and whether the BOJ signals faster normalization while inflation remains elevated. Goldman says today’s move is certain and the next hike could come as early as December; high energy, strong AI-driven demand, a weaker yen and loose fiscal policy could push inflation and yields higher. TD Securities projects a path to 2.25% via hikes in Dec and several 2027 moves and warns that omission of Oct/Dec hikes from guidance could send USD/JPY to 157–160. Market pricing already implies ~90bp of tightening over the next 12 months; if Governor Ueda signals caution, that could leave the yen exposed. Overall market takeaway: 25bp hike probable; guidance tone will be the primary driver for near-term yen moves, JGB yields and expectations for further BOJ tightening.

### 2026-09-18 08:59:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Australia #Currency #Importance 1

Australian Dollar Attempts Rebound — The Australian dollar rose above $0.71, attempting to recover from a recent four-week low as hawkish comments from the Reserve Bank’s top official reinforced expectations for further policy tightening. Governor Michele Bullock said upside inflation risks were materializing and warned that higher costs could make inflation more persistent, pointing to rising energy prices and continued capacity pressures in the domestic economy. Her remarks follow statements over the past month by senior RBA officials that inflation remains elevated and needs to ease further. Swaps markets now priced at least two more rate hikes by the February meeting, with a roughly 87% chance the first occurs on September 29. That marks a notable shift from the start of September, when traders were pricing just one further hike, likely by year-end. The Aussie also benefited from a softer US dollar, alongside lower Treasury yields and easing oil prices, while a rebound in global equities improved risk sentiment.

Meanwhile, Japan’s annual inflation rate held steady at 1.9% in August, while core inflation stayed below the BOJ’s 2% target for a seventh straight month at 1.7%. Silver Holds Gains on Lower Oil Prices — Silver held above $65 an ounce on Friday after climbing more than 3% in the previous session, supported by falling oil prices that eased inflation concerns and helped push bond yields lower. Oil prices declined for a third consecutive session as Saudi Arabia worked to restore flows through its East-West pipeline, while President Donald Trump is set to meet with Gulf leaders next week. Treasury yields also pulled back from multi-year highs, with the US 10-year yield falling to around 4.93% after briefly exceeding 5% earlier this week. Meanwhile, investors continued to assess the outlook for Federal Reserve monetary policy following its first rate hike in three years. The US central bank signaled that additional tightening could be needed to contain inflationary pressures, with markets now pricing in roughly a 53% probability of a move in October.

【CITIC Securities: gas-turbine industry in new upcycle】CITIC Securities says the gas-turbine industry is in a new upcycle. Demand for small- and mid-sized turbines is rising faster owing to flexible deployment and shorter construction cycles. Overseas OEM capacity expansion has long lead times, keeping supply tight; bottlenecks in hot-end components and large rotating parts are transmitting upstream. Overseas supply gaps and constrained deliveries of core engines for aviation fuel reform are likely to shift demand to domestic complete-machine and component makers, boosting demand for upstream materials such as high-temperature alloys.

### 2026-09-18 08:09:53  #Trading Economics #Economy #Japan #Food Inflation #Importance 1

Japan Food Prices Rise the Least in 25 Months — Japan’s food prices rose 3.1% year-on-year in August 2026, slowing from 3.5% in the previous month and marking the slowest increase since July 2024. Price pressures moderated across fresh vegetables (5.5% vs 6.5% in July), fresh fruit (0.1% vs 2.4%), dairy products and eggs (1.3% vs 3.5%), cakes and candies (5.0% vs 6.1%), meats (3.3% vs 3.9%), alcoholic beverages (0.4% vs 0.6%), and prepared foods (3.3% vs 3.5%). Meanwhile, cereal prices fell for a fourth straight month (-3.4% vs -2.2%). At the same time, rice prices dropped 15.7% year-on-year, extending their decline for a fourth consecutive month and marking the steepest fall since April 2005. In contrast, prices accelerated for fish and seafood (9.9% vs 9.0%), oils, fats and seasonings (4.0% vs 3.4%), beverages (6.0% vs 5.5%), and meals eaten out (2.1% vs 1.7%).

【Huatai Securities: Big three airlines' August load factors strong; sector rebound awaits oil-price decline】Huatai Securities reports that in August peak travel the big three carriers (Air China, China Eastern, China Southern) plus Spring Airlines and Juneyao saw ASK +5.2% YoY and RPK +7.6% YoY; load factor rose 2ppt to a record 88.8% and ticket pricing showed some improvement. However, July–August domestic fares including fuel surcharges rose only 1.0% YoY—extreme weather and demand/capacity mismatches limited pricing and weakened oil-price pass-through. Huatai says a sector rebound will await lower oil prices; monitor high-frequency Mid-Autumn and National Day travel data. It expects cautious capacity deployment in Q4, which could support YoY revenue performance, and remains constructive on a mid-to-long-term recovery in the transport sector.

US Futures Steady After Upbeat Session — US stock futures were little changed on Friday after the major averages rallied in the previous session, as falling oil prices and bond yields improved risk appetite. In regular trading on Thursday, the Dow rose 0.61%, the S&P 500 gained 1.14%, and the Nasdaq Composite advanced 1.69%. Chipmakers led the gains, extending their recovery after concerns over AI safety earlier in the week sparked doubts about AI-related capital spending. Strong performances came from Micron (5.5%), Nvidia (2.5%), Intel (7.7%), AMD (6.4%), and SanDisk (6.2%). Stocks benefited from lower oil prices that eased inflation worries and helped Treasuries rally across the curve, with the US 10-year yield falling to around 4.93% after topping 5% earlier this week. Wall Street also rallied in relief after the Federal Reserve delivered a widely expected rate hike, though policymakers signaled further tightening to curb inflation.

【日本通脹率四個月來首次回落 日本央行即將公佈利率決策】日本通脹率四個月來首次回落，主要反映政府補貼的影響。在消費者價格(CPI)資料公佈數小時後，日本央行將公佈利率決策。日本政府週五公佈，8月不含生鮮食品的CPI同比上漲1.7%，低於7月的1.8%。經濟學家此前預計漲幅持平。作為日本央行密切關注的通脹指標，剔除生鮮食品和能源的CPI同比上漲1.9%。整體CPI亦上漲1.9%。接受媒體調查的經濟學家一致預計，日本央行週五將加息25個基點至1.25%。這將是該央行三個月內第二次加息，緊縮步伐勢創1990年以來最快。

Japan Core Inflation Falls Below Forecasts — Japan’s core consumer price index, which excludes fresh food but includes energy, increased 1.7% year-on-year in August 2026, after a 1.8% rise in July. The latest reading slowed from the highest rate in six months, touched a month earlier, and was below the market forecast of 1.8%. Still, the latest reading remained below the Bank of Japan’s 2% target for an eighth straight month, as government fuel subsidies helped offset price pressures from higher oil costs linked to the Middle East conflict.

Brent Slides for Third Session — Brent crude fell to around $104 per barrel on Friday, extending its decline for a third consecutive session as concerns over supply disruptions in the Middle East eased and hopes grew for renewed diplomatic efforts to end the conflict and restore energy flows. Saudi Arabia is targeting the recovery of around half the capacity of its East-West pipeline within days, with a return to full operations expected within six weeks. In the meantime, the kingdom is rerouting some crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz. Shuttle vessels transport crude through Hormuz before loading tankers waiting outside the strait, limiting their exposure to potential Iranian attacks. Reports also suggested that China had urged Iran to help curb Houthi militants after an appeal from Riyadh, as the rebel group intensified attacks on Saudi energy infrastructure. Meanwhile, President Donald Trump said he was weighing whether to resume attacks on Iran ahead of a meeting with Gulf leaders in New York next week.

Costa Rica Unemployment Rate Falls to 7.0% in Q2 — The unemployment rate in Costa Rica fell to 7.0% in the second quarter of 2026 from 7.4% in the same period a year earlier, but little changed from 7.1% in the previous quarter. Nationwide, the number of unemployed people was estimated at 164,000, including 98,000 men and 66,000 women. The unemployment rate was 6.8% for men and 7.2% for women. The labor force stood at 2.35 million, comprising 1.43 million men and 921,000 women, while the net labor force participation rate was 54.6%, at 66.3% for men and 42.9% for women. Employment rose to 2.18 million, with the employment rate at 50.8%, compared with 61.7% for men and 39.8% for women. Informal employment totaled 785,000, including 438,000 self-employed workers and 348,000 wage earners or family helpers.

New Zealand Equities Head for Weekly Gain — The NZX 50 advanced 29 points, or 0.2%, to 13,785 in Friday morning trade, up for the third straight session, tracking gains on Wall Street overnight as easing oil prices softened Treasury yields, while the Fed’s rate hike reaffirmed the central bank’s commitment to contain inflation. Thursday’s data continued to lift sentiment after New Zealand’s GDP grew faster than expected in Q2. However, traders awaited China’s interest rate decision and US PMI data next week for clues about the economic outlook in the two countries. On the data front, New Zealand’s trade deficit narrowed in August from July, as exports rose more than imports, while food inflation stayed at a 19-month low. Real estate, consumer discretionary, and financials mainly drove the index, with notable gains including Port of Tauranga (1.0%), Chorus (0.8%), Hallenstein Glasson (0.8%), and Auckland International Airport (0.5%).

New Zealand Food Inflation Stays at 19-Month Low — Annual food inflation in New Zealand stood at 1.9% in August 2026, unchanged from the previous month, which was the softest increase since December 2024. Prices for meat, poultry, and fish increased by 3.4% (vs 3.2% in July), after marking the slowest rate since at least June 2025 in July, and remained the largest contributor to the annual increase in food prices. Grocery food prices also accelerated (1.7% vs 1.6% in July), while fruit and vegetable prices fell at a faster rate, declining 1.6%, following a 1.0% drop in July.

### 2026-09-18 06:54:22  #公司

【中小股東提案門檻要“降”，行權通道更要“暢”】新《公司法》將股東臨時提案持股門檻降至1%，至今已一年有餘，然而門檻下降並未明顯啟用中小股東的提案熱情。中小股東提案少，並非投資者沒有訴求。近兩年多場上市公司股東會上，關聯交易、股權激勵、修訂規則等方案遭遇中小股東高票反對，部分議案在大股東迴避表決後直接被否決。這說明，中小投資者對公司經營、利益分配有著清晰判斷，參與公司治理的意願持續高漲。可現實是，很多中小股東的合理訴求難以轉化為正式提案，制度供給與實際行權之間仍存在落差。行權成本高是第一道現實門檻。股東行使臨時提案權，需單獨或合計持有1%股份。對於分散股東而言，想要聯合湊齊這一持股比例，會面臨股東資訊隔離、聯絡渠道匱乏等難題，且自發聯合的時間成本、溝通成本高昂。即便湊夠持股條件，相關提案還需要具備清晰議題、可付諸表決的決議事項。普通投資者大多不熟悉相關規則，又缺少專業中介力量支援，不少提案因為格式、文字等形式瑕疵，直接被董事會擋在股東會之外。（證券時報）

【“月付退出支付選項”系誤讀，記者實測：收銀臺頁面正在“分家”】近日，“平臺月付將退出支付選項”登上微博熱搜，相關話題不斷髮酵，引發消費者對選擇支付方式的疑問：以後花唄、美團月付、京東白條等產品不能用於線上付款了？日前，記者從支付寶、京東、美團等多家平臺客服處瞭解到，月付類產品仍可正常使用，且平臺會遵照監管規定對收銀臺頁面的展示方式進行調整。業內人士分析稱，上述熱搜中的說法是對《金融產品網路營銷管理辦法》第十二條規定的誤讀。新規並非禁止貸款類產品用於支付，而是強調要將此類產品與銀行卡、賬戶餘額等基礎支付工具區隔展示，從源頭上治理支付環節誘導借貸、“無感借貸”等亂象。（中證報）

【Anthropic披露AI發展指標：Claude主導公司26%研發 參與約90%工作】Anthropic PBC表示，該公司超過四分之一的人工智慧研發工作由Claude聊天機器人主導，這為AI能夠幫助加快未來模型開發提供了迄今最明確的跡象。根據Anthropic週四釋出的報告，Claude“主導”該公司26%的研發，而今年年初這一比例幾乎為零。這家AI開發商表示，Claude還參與協助員工約90%的工作。這些資料是Anthropic更廣泛報告的一部分。隨著外界日益擔憂AI可能迅速提升自身能力並脫離人類控制，該報告旨在幫助公眾追蹤AI的發展進展。該公司還重申，計劃引入來自多家機構的第三方評估人員，並向其授予與本公司員工同等的內部流程、系統及資料訪問許可權。“AI系統正以指數級速度變得更加強大，並已開始將更多構建自身的過程自動化。隨著全球考慮放慢前沿AI的開發速度，公眾需要獲得更多資訊，”該公司寫道。

### 2026-09-18 05:23:07  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Brent Extends Drop as Supply Risks Ease — Brent crude oil settled near $104 a barrel on Thursday, extending a 2.7% drop from the previous session, as Saudi Arabia shifts some crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz to offset the closure of a key pipeline. Saudi Arabia is reportedly making additional crude cargoes available to Asian refiners through ship-to-ship transfers near Oman’s Sohar port. Shuttle vessels transport crude through Hormuz before loading tankers waiting outside the strait, reducing their exposure to Iranian attacks. Saudi Arabia also said it could restore around half of the damaged East-West pipeline’s capacity within days and full operations within six weeks. Saudi crude loadings at Mideast Gulf ports have risen this month, while ship-to-ship transfers in the Gulf of Oman increased to 2.7 million bpd from 1.5 million bpd in August. Meanwhile, Russia’s seaborne oil product exports jumped 16.4% in August from July as refineries returned from maintenance.

South Korea Producer Inflation Rises in August — South Korea’s producer prices rose 7.9% year over year in August 2026, accelerating from a 7.7% increase in July. Ibovespa Edges Higher After Selic Cut — The Ibovespa edged 0.2% higher to close at 185,992 on Thursday after a volatile session following the BCB’s decision to cut the Selic rate by 25 bps to 13.75%. The Copom did not signal its next steps but left the door open for another cut at the next meeting. The message was one of continuity, with monetary policy remaining cautious, data-dependent, and focused on composure. Still, the index fell as much as 1.2% during the session amid concerns over government spending and electoral effects after the federal government announced a 15% increase in welfare benefits. Polls showing challenger Flávio Bolsonaro numerically ahead in second-round scenarios continued to support the index. A halt in the oil rally and the consequent softening of global yields also helped support the market. Major banks were mixed, with Banco do Brasil rising 0.3% and Santander advancing 2.5%, while Itaú inched lower and Bradesco fell 0.3%. Vale rose 2.1%, helped by higher iron ore prices.

TSX Closes Higher on Oil Pullback — Argentina’s unemployment rate rose to 7.9% in the second quarter of 2026, up from 7.8% in the previous quarter, although the change was not statistically significant. The employment rate edged up to 45.0% from 44.8%, while labor force participation increased to 48.9% from 48.6%. By region, unemployment was highest in the Pampas region at 9.7%, followed by Greater Buenos Aires at 8.2%. The jobless rate stood at 8.4% for women and 7.5% for men. Unemployment among women aged 14 to 29 reached 17.1%, while the rate for men in the same age group stood at 14.1%. By education level, the largest share of unemployment was among those with completed secondary education at 2.8% of the labor force. Regarding job-search duration, 2.4% of the labor force had been looking for work for more than a year. Meanwhile, unemployment in urban areas outside Greater Buenos Aires rose significantly, from 6.8% to 7.6%.

【NBC: Pentagon weighing withdrawal of about 25,000 U.S. troops from Europe】NBC, citing seven current and former U.S. and Western officials, reports the Pentagon is assessing a significant drawdown of U.S. forces in Europe that could withdraw roughly 25,000 troops—about one-third of the roughly 80,000 currently stationed there—and could extend to as many as 40,000. The Pentagon launched a six-month review in June; an assessment is expected to be submitted to the defense secretary soon. Options under consideration include cuts to air, naval and ground personnel or relocation of some units. Officials say the review aims to align deployments with the "America First" strategy. Some European governments and U.S. lawmakers warn a large-scale withdrawal could weaken NATO deterrence; supporters argue for rebalancing overseas forces and shifting greater defense burden to European allies. No decision has been made; any withdrawal would require presidential approval.

Argentina GDP Growth Slows in Q2 — Argentina’s economy grew 2.0% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, slowing from a 2.3% increase in the previous quarter, but beating forecasts of a 1.6% expansion. The strongest sector gains came from fishing (44.7%), mining and quarrying (16.4%), and agriculture, livestock, hunting and forestry (6.9%). Other notable expansions were recorded in electricity, gas and water (5.0%), financial intermediation (4.8%), and hotels and restaurants (1.6%). In contrast, manufacturing contracted 2.1%, while public administration and defense declined 1.4%, marking the weakest performances. On the demand side, exports rose 13.7% year-on-year, leading growth, while private consumption increased 0.4% and public consumption fell 4.1%. Gross fixed capital formation contracted 11.1%, while imports of goods and services fell 8.6%. On a quarter-on-quarter basis, the economy contracted 0.6% in seasonally adjusted terms.

McVey為首的團隊寫道：“我們仍然認為，在名義增長處於高位、財政赤字龐大以及對資本的競爭持續的環境下，收益率曲線長端的投資者將要求獲得合理的期限溢價。”

【特朗普政府據悉考慮設立網路安全孵化器 資助研發並培育初創企業】據知情人士透露，特朗普政府正在評估一項方案，計劃成立由政府主導的孵化器，旨在投資網路安全研究，並孵化專注於為政府開發工具的初創企業。因討論未公開訊息而要求匿名的知情人士表示，白宮國家網路總監辦公室在起草一項行政命令，擬設立一個專案來研究和開發符合美國政府優先事項的網路技術。該專案旨在吸引私人風險投資資金，在行政令草案的一個版本中，該專案被稱為“網路鑄造廠”（cyber foundry），將孵化新的企業。據熟悉相關計劃的人士透露，美國政府內部將這一構想與以色列的做法相比較，後者將軍事優先事項與國內網路安全初創企業、風險投資和學術研究的重點緊密結合。Wiz、派拓網路公司和Check Point軟體技術有限公司等多家知名網路安全公司均由以色列網路情報部門前成員創立。

### 2026-09-18 02:56:11  #市場 #觀點 #國際

【摩根大通關於伊朗戰爭以何種方式結束：“我們根本不知道”】伊朗戰爭爆發六個多月後，摩根大通的石油分析師公開表達了許多交易員一段時間以來私下所說的看法：這場戰爭將以何種方式結束，正變得越來越難以預測。包括Natasha Kaneva在內的分析師在一份報告中表示，該行此前認為美國政府不願突破的幾條經濟紅線，包括油價升至每桶100美元以上、汽油價格逼近每加侖5美元以及美國國債收益率飆升，如今都已經出現，這使得退出戰略變得更加不明朗。隨著能源價格飆升，而由此產生的連鎖影響可能破壞全球經濟增長並加劇通脹，石油交易員和分析師越來越不確定伊朗戰爭還會持續多久。包括沙烏地阿拉伯至關重要的東西向輸油管道在內的關鍵能源基礎設施近期遭到襲擊，加劇了市場對供應進一步趨緊的擔憂。分析師們在週四被市場參與者廣泛援引的一份報告中表示：“我們認為，市場目前高度緊張。” 他們表示，9月份石油的合理價值估計約為每桶90美元，儘管目前價格接近106美元，並補充說，這意味著，在目前已經有每日1000萬桶供應受到干擾的基礎上，市場正在計入每日額外損失400萬桶供應的風險。

【US approves PEZESHKIAN and Iran foreign minister to attend UN General Assembly in New York】The U.S. State Department told AP it has approved travel for Iranian President PEZESHKIAN and Foreign Minister Araghchi to attend next week’s UN General Assembly high-level meetings in New York. The department said Iran’s core delegation and necessary staff will be allowed to participate but will face travel restrictions and a U.S. policy ban on purchasing luxury and other restricted items; the delegation will be smaller than last year. The decision comes after more than six months of U.S.-Iran conflict, with no material progress on reopening the Strait of Hormuz or restarting Iran nuclear talks. As host of the UN, the U.S. is constrained by host-country obligations and has previously issued limited visas and activity curbs for representatives from conflict parties. The approval follows a separate U.S. visa denial for Palestinian President Mahmoud Abbas one day earlier.

【中方強調維護伊核問題政治解決大局】當地時間9月17日上午，在聯合國安理會會議上，美國等國無視涉及伊朗核問題的第2231號決議已於2025年10月18日到期終止，安理會已終止審議伊朗核問題，在安理會強推恢復對伊朗制裁、提出明顯具有爭議的決議草案。中國和俄羅斯在對該草案的表決中投下反對票，巴基斯坦和索馬利亞投下棄權票，草案未獲透過。 中國常駐聯合國代表傅聰大使在表決後的解釋性發言中表示，中方從捍衛公道正義、維護中東地區和平穩定出發，著眼伊朗核問題政治外交解決大局，對決議草案投了反對票。 傅聰大使指出，形勢越是動盪，安理會越要展現領導力，以負責任態度採取行動，避免雙重標準、政治操弄、施壓對抗。 傅聰大使強調，軍事手段和制裁施壓無助於解決問題，只會加劇緊張，升級局勢不符合任何一方利益，平等對話談判才是唯一正途。 據瞭解，這一被否決的決議草案尋求將根據安理會第1929號決議（2010年）設立、將於9月26日屆滿的伊朗制裁委專家小組任期延長一年。該專家小組負責監督對伊朗制裁制度的執行情況。（CCTV國際時訊）

【摩根大通：根本不知道伊朗戰爭何時結束，經濟紅線已現】摩根大通石油分析師稱，“我們根本不知道” 伊朗戰爭何時結束  ，戰爭結束道路越來越難以預測。該行此前認為美國政府不會跨越的多條經濟紅線均已出現，包括油價突破每桶100美元、汽油價格接近每加侖5美元以及國債收益率飆升，令退出策略更不明朗。分析師娜塔莎·卡內瓦等人指出，油價目前接近每桶106美元，而9月原油合理價格估計約為每桶90美元，意味著市場正計入額外400萬桶/日的供應損失風險，此前已造成每日1000萬桶供應中斷。摩根大通稱，包括沙特東-西向輸油管道在內的關鍵能源基礎設施最近遭攻擊，加劇供應進一步緊張擔憂，市場情緒緊張。分析師認為，美國和伊朗均未發出準備緩和局勢的明確訊號，因此認為當前混亂只是暫時的假設越來越難以維持。

### 2026-09-18 00:58:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Lumber Falls to Nine-Month Low — Lumber futures fell below $550 per thousand board feet, reaching their lowest level in nine months, as elevated borrowing costs continued to weigh on demand. The US housing market remains tight, with mortgage rates near their highest level in over a year and showing no sign of easing after the Federal Reserve hiked its key policy rate, weighing on demand from prospective homebuyers. Building permits also fell 2.7% over the month, although a 7.6% increase in lumber-intensive single-family housing starts in August. Provided an offsetting pressure, US-Canada trade talks fell through, raising concerns over lumber supply. Ottawa had announced counter-tariffs of 25% on US lumber and 50% on plywood following US tariffs on several lumber-related goods. US forestry groups have long accused Canada of distorting the market, noting that 94% of Canadian forestland is publicly owned, compared with 58% privately owned in the US, allowing Canadian lumber to be sold at lower prices.

European Stocks Extend Rebound — European stocks closed firmly higher on Thursday, tracking the positive momentum for stocks worldwide after a pullback in oil and gas prices softened the elevated level for sovereign yields. The Euro STOXX 50 gained 0.9% to 6,323 and the STOXX Europe 600 rose 0.9% to 643. Natural gas benchmarks in the continent and wholesale fuel costs eased after Saudi Arabia signaled its East-West pipeline could return to operation shortly, softening risks of even more aggressive inflation in the Eurozone. Meanwhile, the Fed raised its rates by 25bps, as expected, but the hawkish tone in Chairman Warsh's remarks flattened yield curves across economies with exposure to US credit, also softening long borrowing costs. Banks closed higher, with Santander, BBVA, and Deutsche Bank adding close to 2%. Power-hungry industrials also gained, with Siemens Energy, Schneider, and Siemens jumping between 3.5% and 2.5%.

【奈及利亞翁多州疑似甲醇中毒事件死亡人數升至48人】奈及利亞翁多州政府17日稱，該州居民飲用當地釀造的酒精飲料後疑似中毒事件持續蔓延，死亡人數已升至48人。 翁多州政府稱，該州兩個社群9月初接連出現居民飲用當地釀造的酒精飲料後就醫的情況，10日已報告至少20人死亡。此後事件持續蔓延，截至目前，當地醫療機構已報告超過182例相關病例，其中48人死亡。 當地訊息人士稱，一名本地釀酒者將這種酒精飲料出售給當地居民，其中大多數為年輕人。 當地政府稱，受害者的尿液樣本檢測結果顯示，此次事件疑似與攝入甲醇中毒有關。截至目前，警方已逮捕15名涉嫌生產、銷售該酒精飲料的人員。 當地專家警告稱，甲醇是一種易燃且有毒的醇類物質，不可飲用，人體攝入後可能導致中樞神經系統抑制、永久性失明或死亡。(新華社)

Palladium Holds Steady — Palladium futures held near $1,300 per ounce, their lowest level in over a month, following the Federal Reserve’s monetary policy meeting. As widely expected, the Fed hiked its benchmark rate for the first time since 2023 and is expected to deliver another hike this year amid elevated economic and geopolitical uncertainty, raising the opportunity cost of holding non-yielding assets such as palladium. Meanwhile, UBS forecasts a market surplus for palladium, as increased recycling and weaker consumption of catalytic converters are expected to more than offset lower mining output compared with last year. Persistent supply vulnerabilities in South Africa, linked to elevated operating costs and power grid instability, continued to raise concerns over mined output, while ongoing trade and geopolitical risks affecting Russian palladium also provided a supportive backdrop for prices.

【Yaroslavl refinery halts crude processing after Ukrainian drone strike】Four industry sources said Slavneft’s Yaroslavl refinery (YANOS) halted crude processing on Thursday after a Ukrainian drone strike damaged equipment. Yaroslavl region governor Mikhail Yevrayev said authorities had foiled a large-scale drone attack: "65 drones were shot down, no casualties. The refinery was damaged and firefighting is ongoing." President Zelenskiy said Ukraine struck the refinery overnight. The plant is about 250 km (155 miles) northeast of Moscow. Sources said the strike damaged the main crude distillation unit AVT-3, with design throughput of 17,140 tonnes/day, roughly 40% of the refinery’s capacity. The refinery’s other major units are AT-4 (11,430 t/d, 27%) and AVT-4 (14,300 t/d, 33%); AVT-4 was already damaged in an Aug. 28 drone attack and remains under repair. Market participants said fuels from the Yaroslavl plant were not listed on the Russian domestic commodity exchange on Thursday.

【French prime minister proposes €54bln fiscal consolidation to curb deficit widening】French Prime Minister Sébastien Lecornu proposed a roughly €54 billion fiscal consolidation plan to restrain budget spending growth through 2027 and prevent further deficit widening. He warned that if next year’s finance bill fails to curb persistent spending growth, the deficit could rise from 5.1% of GDP in 2025 to as much as 6.5%. France faces slowing growth, a split parliament and rising resistance to spending cuts ahead of a presidential election in about seven months, with the opposition unwilling to cooperate. A recent global bond sell-off has pushed the French 10-year OAT spread over German Bunds to about 96 bps, near euro-area crisis highs. The government has cut this year’s growth forecast to 0.5%, below the 0.9% assumed in the 2026 budget; Finance Minister Roland Lescure said the original 5% of GDP deficit target “is no longer feasible.”

Brazil 10-Year Yield Falls After Selic Cut — Brazil's 10-year government bond yield fell to 14.25% from the two-week high of 14.5% on September 15th amid a pullback in energy prices and rate cut by the Central Bank of Brazil. The BCB cut its rate by 25bps to 13.75% and left its next steps open, saying that the current environment of uncertainty calls for composure and caution in the conduct of monetary policy. It was the final monetary policy meeting before the presidential election in October. Yields were also pressured by a pullback in oil prices, limiting the risks of a hawkish turn by the central bank. Meanwhile, gains by Flávio Bolsonaro in recent presidential election polls have supported Brazilian bonds, as markets view Bolsonaro as fiscally restrictive, while elevated domestic yields and weak business activity continue to weigh on the economic outlook.

### 2026-09-18 00:03:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Sugar Futures at Near 1-Month Low — US sugar futures have been under pressure, trading near one-month lows of 18.4 cents per pound, mainly pressured by lower oil prices. Softer crude oil prices reduce the competitiveness of ethanol, encouraging mills to allocate a smaller share of sugarcane to biofuel production. Meanwhile, the global sugar supply outlook remains tight, with recent estimates pointing to a potential deficit in 2026/27 as production is expected to decline among key suppliers. El Niño poses an additional risk to global sugar production, with a strong event potentially bringing drier conditions to major producers such as Brazil, India and Thailand. In Brazil, the world’s largest producer and exporter, crop conditions and the split of sugarcane between sugar and ethanol remain important drivers of prices. Ethanol prices at mills in São Paulo state rose more than 2% last week as heavy rains delayed crushing and raised concerns about the volume of cane that can be processed during the 2026/27 season.

The central bank warned that rates could need to rise if inflationary pressures intensify as a result of the conflict in the Middle East. Six of the nine policymakers, including Governor Andrew Bailey, voted to maintain the current rate, leaving the committee split along the same lines as in July. South Africa 10-Year Bond Yield at Over 1-Week Low — South Africa’s 10-year government bond yield fell to around 8.80%, its lowest in over a week, tracking a global decline, amid easing oil prices that alleviated some inflation concerns. Another key catalyst was the Federal Reserve’s decision to hike interest rates, with Chair Warsh reaffirming the central bank’s commitment to tackling inflation and reinforcing investor confidence in its policy credibility. Meanwhile, the Fed's move raised expectations for a similar move by the South African Reserve Bank (SARB), but economists remain divided over whether the central bank will raise rates or hold next week. The SARB will face another close call, with inflation on an upward trajectory and a weakening economy complicating the policy outlook. South Africa’s consumer inflation rate fell to 4.3% in July from 5% in June, though it is expected to rise again in August and September due to higher oil and fuel prices. The focus now turns to August inflation data, due on September 23.

### 2026-09-17 23:32:35  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Rise After EIA Data — US natural gas prices rose to $2.92 per million British thermal units after EIA data showed that high temperatures and strong air-conditioning demand limited the amount of gas added to storage last week. Energy firms injected 44 billion cubic feet into storage in the week ended September 11, below the 87 bcf increase a year earlier and the five-year average of 74 bcf. Total inventories reached 3.298 trillion cubic feet, around 3.6% below last year’s level but 3.7% above the five-year average. Meanwhile, forecasts indicate warmer-than-normal weather could persist through October 2, supporting gas demand from power generators. Average flows to the nine major US LNG export facilities were expected to fall to a three-week low of 17.5 bcfd due mainly to maintenance at Cameron LNG in Louisiana. On the supply side, Lower 48 production averaged 113.1 bcfd in September, above August’s record monthly average of 112.2 bcfd.

US Natural Gas Stocks Rise Less Than Expected — US natural gas storage increased by 44 billion cubic feet in the week ended September 11, 2026, slightly below market expectations of a 49 bcf injection. The build was significantly smaller than the 87 bcf increase recorded during the same week last year and the five-year average injection of 74 bcf for the period from 2021 to 2025. Total working gas in storage rose to 3.298 trillion cubic feet, leaving inventories around 3.6% below levels recorded in the corresponding week a year earlier but approximately 3.7% above the five-year average. The latest data followed a 40 bcf injection in the previous week ended September 4.

【阿拉比卡咖啡豆期貨連跌三日 巴西增產預期推動庫存重建】阿拉比卡咖啡豆期貨連續第三個交易日下跌，交投最活躍合約週四跌1.9%，降至6月下旬以來最低水平；羅布斯塔期貨在倫敦市場跌幅達1.3%。市場下跌主因是全球最大咖啡種植國巴西產量增加預期推動庫存重建，此前因降雨擾亂巴西收成而引發的價格上漲壓力已基本消退。國際咖啡組織預測，2025-26年度全球咖啡產量將達到1.836億袋（每袋60公斤），較上年增長4.4%。巴西在洲際交易所（ICE）的認證咖啡庫存已升至23,920袋，為4月中旬以來最高水平。荷蘭合作銀行農產品研究主管卡洛斯·梅拉指出，隨著產量增加，阿拉比卡咖啡豆庫存已出現明顯重建跡象，對價格形成壓力。阿拉比卡咖啡豆相對於羅布斯塔咖啡豆的溢價已從月初的每磅1.51美元收窄至約1.24美元，套利空間大幅縮小。J. Ganes Consulting總裁朱迪·蓋恩斯表示，此前被視為利好的因素現已消失，咖啡價格已回落至恐慌性上漲前的水平。

【190億美元10年期美債TIPS拍賣在即 收益率有望創2008年以來新高】美國財政部將於紐約時間週三下午1點拍賣190億美元10年期通脹保值債券（TIPS），該債券收益率有望創下2008年金融危機以來的最高水平。目前10年期TIPS收益率約為2.63%，週三盤中一度觸及2.69%，為2008年11月以來最高；7月同類拍賣中標收益率達2.438%，已創下當時近18年紀錄，本次拍賣成交價預計較7月發行價再高約0.5個基點。自年中油價回升以來，TIPS相對錶現優於傳統名義國債，但週三美聯儲加息後其表現一度落後，週四隨油價下跌有所反彈。今年以來，10年期TIPS與名義國債收益率分別上升41個基點和48個基點，二者利差（即10年期盈虧平衡通脹率）從2.23%擴大至2.42%，週四回落至2.31%附近。摩根大通利率策略師指出，高實際收益率、合理估值以及持續流入抗通脹資產的資金，應有助於支撐本次債券拍賣。 最新資料顯示，8月美國CPI同比上漲3.4%，核心CPI上漲2.4%，均與市場普遍預期相符。 截至9月2日當週，TIPS一級交易商淨多頭頭寸為29.2億美元，略低於過去一年30億美元的平均水平。

### 2026-09-17 23:03:59  #Trading Economics #Markets #Brazil #Currency #Importance 1

Brazilian Real Steady After Selic Cut — The Brazilian real traded near R$5.15 per US dollar in September, little changed after the BCB cut the Selic rate by 25 basis points to 13.75%. The BCB left its next steps open, saying that the current environment of uncertainty calls for composure and caution in the conduct of monetary policy. It was the final monetary policy meeting before the presidential election in October. The decision was announced after the US Federal Reserve raised its federal funds target rate by 25 basis points to a range of 3.75% to 4.00%. The narrowing interest-rate differential is a factor supporting the dollar, although it had already been priced into markets, with both rate decisions coming in line with expectations. Meanwhile, gains by Flávio Bolsonaro in recent presidential election polls have supported the real, as markets view Bolsonaro as fiscally restrictive, while elevated domestic yields and weak business activity continue to weigh on the economic outlook.

---

### 2026-09-17 23:03:21  #Trading Economics #Markets #South Africa #Currency #Importance 0

South African Rand Firms — The South African rand strengthened toward 16.2 per USD, supported by a softer dollar and firmer precious metals prices on the back of easing oil prices. Meanwhile, traders continued to monitor central bank actions. The Federal Reserve delivered an expected 25 bps rate hike and signalled that further tightening may follow amid persistent inflatonary pressures from the Middle East. Expectations for a SARB rate hike increased after the Fed's move, as a narrower yield differential could weigh on the rand and fuel inflationary pressures. The South African Reserve Bank will face a delicate balancing act when it decides on policy on September 23. Inflation has been rising and, aside from a brief dip in July following fuel-price cuts, remains on an upward trajectory. Meanwhile, the economy slipped back into contraction in the second quarter. At the same time, inflation expectations fell in Q3, signaling that price pressures are expected to ease over time.

### 2026-09-17 22:56:55  #市場 #國際

【美國二手房簽約量意外小幅上升 高利率仍抑制需求】美國二手房簽約量在8月小幅回升，但整體仍然低迷，因抵押貸款利率高企繼續抑制需求。美國全國房地產經紀人協會（NAR）週四公佈的資料顯示，8月二手房簽約量指數上升0.3%，至71.2，為5月以來首次上升。彭博調查的經濟學家此前預計該指數下降0.1%。“儘管抵押貸款利率上升，買家8月仍穩步簽署購房合同，”NAR首席經濟學家Lawrence Yun在宣告中表示。“不過，住房市場依然低迷，簽約量不及去年。”與上年同期相比，二手房簽約量下降近5%。這些資料表明，被壓抑的購房需求依然存在，一些原本可能等待抵押貸款利率下降的買家不再等待，選擇購房。不過，幾乎沒有跡象顯示住房市場在回暖。美國抵押貸款銀行家協會(MBA)的資料顯示，自與伊朗的戰爭開始以來，抵押貸款利率持續攀升，截至9月11日當週一度接近7%，而且利率還可能進一步走高。住房貸款成本與美國10年期國債收益率密切相關，後者本週早些時候升至近20年來最高水平。二手房簽約量報告是二手房銷量的領先指標，因為房屋通常會有一兩個月處於簽約狀態，之後才會完成交易。

【美國首次申領失業救濟人數降至19.6萬 持續申領人數亦下降】美國首次申領失業救濟人數降至7月以來最低水平，持續申領人數則降至兩年多來最低，進一步顯示勞動力市場保持穩定。美國勞工部週四公佈的資料顯示，截至9月12日當週，首次申領失業救濟人數減少1萬人，至19.6萬人，為1969年以來最低讀數之一。該統計期包含勞工節，而失業救濟申請資料在節假日前後可能出現較大波動。首次申領失業救濟人數的四周移動均值降至20.325萬人，為五週以來最低。這一指標有助於平滑周度資料的波動。首次申領人數下降可能反映了勞工節以及新學年開學帶來的季節性波動。即便如此，更廣泛的趨勢仍顯示，除少數受到廣泛關注的裁員訊息外，美國整體經濟中的裁員活動依然低迷。持續申領人數在截至9月5日當週降至173萬人，為2024年以來最低水平。

The MoU affirms the significance of China’s global governance initiative and commits both parties to cooperate in line with the initiative’s five core principles to advance implementation in international law, collaborate on drafting and executing international conventions, and promote awareness of a ‘Friends of Global Governance’ group. 【India rice output seen posting largest drop in 16 years】India's rice output is expected to fall this year for the first time in a decade and by the largest margin in 16 years, the All India Rice Exporters Association said. Association chairman B.V. Krishna Rao said reduced monsoon rainfall will cut output by about 10.0 mln tonnes from last year's record 154.0 mln tonnes, a decline of roughly 6.5% and the biggest since 2009-10. India Meteorological Department data show nationwide rainfall since the June monsoon onset is about 15% below normal, with deficits up to 42% in some rice-growing states. Rao said weeks of dry conditions have significantly weakened production prospects in several southern and eastern states.

【AI股拋售已釋放風險 城堡證券Rubner看好美股年末走勢】隨著美股即將進入今年最後一個季度，城堡證券的股票和衍生品策略主管Scott Rubner對接下來的走勢“越來越持建設性看法”。他說，投資者短期內可能會經歷一段顛簸行情，但如果市場疲軟態勢持續到月底，應當利用這一機會重新增持市場核心板塊。市場對人工智慧的情緒已急劇轉為負面，相關倉位也有所降低，這些趨勢從10月份開始應該會朝相反方向發展，首先是科技股，隨後會擴散至更廣泛的市場。“這並不意味著我們認為9月份的疲軟行情已經結束，”Rubner在給客戶的報告中寫道，“臨近月底，供需格局仍然不利，技術面背景依然對股市構成阻力，而且我們仍然認為未來兩週股市可能進一步走低。但市場格局已經開始發生變化。”標普500指數本月迄今已下跌1.8%，較8月中旬高點低3.2%，該指數11個板塊中有9個在9月份下跌。Rubner寫道，對今年餘下時間而言，重要的是，這輪迴撤“仍然表現為板塊輪動，而非無序下跌”。

【OpenAI reported close to solving another Millennium Prize problem】The Information reports OpenAI is reportedly close to solving another Millennium Prize math problem; an informed source said staff expect the next target, the Hodge conjecture, to be resolved in the near term. 【CKGSB Q3 2026 investor survey: listed companies' net profit growth recovers; market confidence lags】Cheung Kong Graduate School of Business (CKGSB) on Sep. 17 released its Q3 2026 Investor Sentiment Survey showing 58.6% of respondents expect A-shares to rise, down 5.2 percentage points from April 2026, even as trading activity continues to increase. The report highlights a notable improvement in corporate earnings: A-share listed companies' net profit TTM YoY, which was negative from June 2022 through March 2026, recovered to 5.9% in June 2026; private firms reported ~23% net profit growth in 2025 and Q1 2026, accelerating to 36.7% in Q2 2026. CKGSB warns the divergence between improving economic fundamentals and weak market sentiment suggests confidence has yet to be restored and that respondents may be underestimating the combined risks from geopolitics, an AI-driven technological shift, climate change and financial vulnerabilities.

### 2026-09-17 22:12:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Pending Home Sales MoM #Importance 2

US Pending Home Sales Miss Expectations — US pending home sales rose 0.3% month over month in August 2026, rebounding from a revised 2.6% decline in July but falling short of market expectations for a 2.0% increase. Sales increased in the South (2.3%) and West (3.0%), partly offsetting declines in the Northeast (-4.2%) and Midwest (-1.6%). On an annual basis, pending home sales fell 4.7%, with all four major US regions recording declines. “Buyers steadily entered into contracts in August even though mortgage rates increased,” said NAR Chief Economist Lawrence Yun. However, he noted that the housing market remains sluggish, with contract signings below last year’s levels as higher mortgage rates offset gains in purchasing power from job and income growth. Yun added that national contract signings are roughly 30% below pre-pandemic levels. Activity peaked in 2021, when mortgage rates fell to near 3%, a historic low, and has not returned to those levels since.

### 2026-09-17 22:07:34  #公司 #市場

【OpenAI有望攻克另一項千禧年大獎數學難題】本月早些時候，數學家指責OpenAI試圖搶佔先機、爭奪一道超高難度數學難題的成果。據瞭解相關研究進展的人士透露，事實是OpenAI距離攻克千禧年大獎難題中的另一道數學難題已近在咫尺。千禧年大獎難題一共包含七大著名數學挑戰，此前引發爭議的納維–斯托克斯方程存在性與光滑性問題正是其中之一。 該訊息人士稱，公司內部員工預計，下一個目標——霍奇猜想，有望在較短時間內得到證明。霍奇猜想研究的是：由多項式方程定義幾何體時，其特定幾何特徵是否總能用更簡單的代陣列件來描述。不過，OpenAI可能需要更長時間才會對外公佈證明結果。原因在於，公司正研究如何與數學界開展合作，以合適的方式釋出成果，避免再次釀成公關危機。

【中信建投：加息開始，美股/商品的順風期或結束】中信建投研究報告表示，政策路徑，年內二度加息機率不低，明年不確定性仍大。沃什全面倒向防通脹，官員強烈傾向繼續加，短期想改變這一緊縮敘事很難，需要極強的宏觀變化和資料支援。但是，2027年大方向還需觀察，經濟資料、工作組結論等變數存在。市場短期謹慎，大類資產集體回撤、或結構分化的機率激增，近3年普漲大牛市（除債券），面臨終結風險。① 宏觀環境不友好，經濟K型（需求不足），AI敘事放緩（美股估值受限），高利率壓制（波動率抬升），且資產價格均在歷史高位，走出趨勢行情不容易。② 過去3年，美股、工業品、貴金屬集體衝高，聯儲寬鬆週期是一大背景，隨著降息結束、加息重啟，各類資產從順風轉為逆風，普漲基礎鬆動，建議等待一輪下跌後，再擇機介入。

### 2026-09-17 21:58:21  #市場 #觀點 #央行 #國際

【中信建投分析師曾羽：沃什首次加息後撤回寬鬆，美債熊平】中信建投分析師曾羽認為，美聯儲9月會議全票加息25bp，加息已被市場充分定價，會議的關鍵資訊在於：宣告刪去通脹的能源供給衝擊歸因，本次刪除與沃什反覆強調的通脹太高、持續太久相呼應，表明委員會已不再將通脹視為可短期消化的供給衝擊。點陣圖與經濟預測同步上修，年內再加一次成為中位數判斷，通脹回到2%目標的時點進一步推遲；沃什表示委員會對通脹正朝目標移動缺乏信心，並延續不提供前瞻指引的溝通風格。市場呈現典型熊平：短端定價更高的政策利率路徑、跳升破位，長端交易的是聯儲控通脹的能力，上行相對溫和。往後看，今年再加一次為基準情形；1999年以來美聯儲從未只加一次，但本輪起點已具限制性、累計空間有限，明年的路徑重度依賴油價與通脹資料。

Rice Futures Hover at 2-Year Highs — Rice futures traded near $16 per hundredweight, holding close to their highest level since July 2024, amid expectations of tighter supply. While global rice supplies remain ample, erratic weather and below-normal monsoon rains in major Asian producers continue to cloud the supply outlook. Top exporter India is on course for its weakest monsoon in nearly two decades in the face of a strengthening El Nino weather phenomenon. The country's rice output is set to fall this year for the first time in a decade, with the decline expected to be the steepest in nearly 20 years.

The aircraft will be operated under a leasing agreement.

Kosovo GDP Growth Slows to 4.06% in Q2 — Kosovo’s economy grew 4.06% year-on-year in the second quarter of 2026, slowing from a downwardly revised 5.27% expansion in the previous quarter. Growth was broad-based across sectors, with wholesale and retail trade, repair of motor vehicles and motorcycles, transport and storage, and accommodation and food service activities rising 3.48%. Financial and insurance activities also increased sharply, by 15.67%. Additional growth came from information and communication (5.04%), arts, entertainment and recreation, household production activities for own use and other service activities (1.29%), scientific, professional and technical activities and administrative and support service activities (3.99%), and manufacturing (3.48%). From the expenditure side, exports of goods and services had the largest impact, rising 6.82%, while gross fixed capital formation increased 11.91%. Government spending rose 7.02%, while household consumption increased 4.4%.

### 2026-09-17 21:30:45  #Trading Economics #Economy #Cape Verde #Inflation Rate #Importance 0

Cape Verde Inflation Rate Inches Higher to 0.8% — The annual inflation rate in Cape Verde rose to 0.8% in August 2026 from 0.6% in the prior month. The main upward pressure came from prices of furnishings & household equipment (4.9% vs 5.9%) in July) and transportation (4.5% vs 2.5%), while alcoholic beverages and tobacco (4.1% vs 3.6%) and hotels, cafes and restaurants (2.8% vs 2.6%) also contributed. By contrast, prices decreased for housing & utilities (-1.8% vs 0.1%); communications (-0.4% vs -0.3%) and food & non-alcoholic beverages (-0.4% vs -0.1%). On a monthly basis, the CPI fell by 0.1% in August, after a 0.2% increase in the preceding month.

### 2026-09-17 21:24:25  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Argentina Consumer Confidence Improves in September — Argentina’s Consumer Confidence Index (ICC) rose 2.37% month over month to 41.8 points in September 2026, following a 1.08% decline in August. Future Expectations increased 3.80%, while Current Conditions rose 0.33%. By subindex, Personal Situation increased 11.33% in September, while the other components declined. The largest decrease was recorded in Durable Goods and Real Estate (-3.42%), followed by Macroeconomic Situation (-0.75%). By region, the ICC increased in Greater Buenos Aires and the rest of the country, while declining in the City of Buenos Aires. The largest increase was recorded in the Interior (+5.23%), followed by GBA (+3.33%), while CABA posted a 6.78% decline. By income level, results were mixed: low-income households recorded a 12.33% increase, while high-income households posted a 4.07% decline. Year-on-year, the index rose 3.44%.

【US August housing starts unexpectedly fall】US housing starts unexpectedly slowed in August, down 2.6% to an annualized 1.28 mln units, one of the weakest readings since the pandemic, government data showed on Thursday. Multifamily starts plunged nearly 22% to an annualized 357k units; single-family starts rose 7.6% to 918k, the fastest monthly gain since March, led by the West and Midwest. Rising mortgage rates and affordability constraints continue to weigh on the market, and housing inventory remains well above pre-pandemic levels, which may keep builders from accelerating new construction.

Baltic Dry Index Snaps 5-Day Losing Run — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, rose by 0.3% to 3,336 points on Thursday, snapping a five-day losing streakthat pushed the index to the lowest since September 1. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, increased by 0.8% to 5,656 points, partly supported by iron ore prices as Chinese steelmakers increased seaborne purchases ahead of a national holiday. At the same time, the supramax index went up 0.8% to 1,762 points. On the other hand, the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell further, down 1.8% to 2,282 points.

Canada Raw Materials Prices Rise More Than Expected — Canada’s raw materials prices rose 3.1% month-on-month in August 2026, rebounding from a 2.1% decline in July and well above market expectations of a 0.7% gain. Prices for crude and energy products were the main driver, surging 7.1% following three consecutive months of declines, as renewed tensions between the US and Iran pushed energy prices higher. Both synthetic crude oil (21.6%) and conventional crude oil (2.8%) recorded monthly increases. Meanwhile, prices for metal ores, concentrates and scrap rebounded, rising 4% after two consecutive monthly declines, driven by higher prices for gold, silver and platinum group metal ores and concentrates (8.1%), marking the first monthly increase since the start of the year. Silver ores, concentrates and mill bullion gained 9.8%, while gold ores, concentrates and mill bullion rose 6.9%. In contrast, prices for animals and animal products fell 3.4%, marking their largest decline since July 2020.

### 2026-09-17 20:45:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Czech Republic #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Czech Central Bank Extends Rate Pause — The Czech National Bank unanimously decided to maintain its two-week repo rate unchanged at 3.75% in September 2026, as expected, pausing for a second straight meeting. Although the annual inflation rate remained below the central bank's target at 1.9% in August, the “elevated” outlook for 2027 warranted “increased caution in monetary policy.” Policymakers cited several sources of inflationary pressure, including resilient domestic demand, strong wage growth, robust credit expansion and an expansionary fiscal stance. They also noted that the base effect from energy subsidies could turn less favourable, reversing some of their earlier downward contribution to inflation. Moreover, the fallout from the Iran conflict and elevated energy prices continued to pose significant risks to the inflation outlook. Looking forward, Governor Ales Michl said the Board will likely consider keeping rates steady or raising them at its November meeting.

### 2026-09-17 20:44:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Investment in Foreign Securities #Importance 1

Canadian Investors Cut Foreign Securities Holdings — Canadian investors reduced their exposure to foreign securities by C$30.6 billion in July 2026, after increasing it by C$35.43 billion in June. They sold an unprecedented C$31.0 billion of US shares, following C$78.1 billion in purchases during the first half of 2026. The July divestment was mainly concentrated in shares of large US technology companies. US share prices, as measured by the S&P 500 Composite Index, edged down in July after recording strong gains earlier in the year. Meanwhile, Canadian investors reduced their holdings of US government bonds by C$5.1 billion, marking a sixth consecutive monthly divestment and bringing the total since January to C$37.3 billion. The overall outflow was partially offset by purchases of US corporate bonds (+C$1.8 billion) and non-US foreign bonds (+C$3.3 billion). In July, US long-term interest rates rose, while the US dollar depreciated against the Canadian dollar.

### 2026-09-17 20:43:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 2

Canada Producer Prices Unexpectedly Rise — Producer prices in Canada rose 1.3% month-on-month in August 2026, accelerating from a downwardly revised 0.3% increase in July and contrasting with market expectations of a flat reading. Renewed tensions in the conflict between the US and Iran pushed energy and petroleum prices higher, which increased 4% over the month, albeit at a slower pace than the 6.2% gain in July. The increase reflected higher prices for refined products (4.6%), particularly diesel (9.6%), followed by light fuel oils (8.5%). Excluding energy and petroleum products, producer prices rose 0.8%. Meanwhile, prices for primary non-ferrous metal products rebounded, rising 4.4% after two consecutive months of declines, driven by gains in unwrought gold, silver and platinum group metals and their alloys (8%). Prices for chemicals and related products rose 2.8%, rebounding from a 6.9% decline in July, led by increases in plastic resins (10.7%) and fertilizers, pesticides and other chemical products (3.0%).

Philadelphia Fed Manufacturing Activity Slows but Beats Expectations — The Philadelphia Fed Manufacturing Index fell to 37.8 in September 2026 from 47.4 in August, but remained well above market expectations of 30.5, pointing to continued expansion in regional manufacturing, albeit at a slower pace. New orders and shipments remained elevated, while the employment index declined but stayed positive, signalling continued job growth. Both price indicators also moved higher after falling in August. Looking ahead, firms remained optimistic about the next six months, with 57.9% expecting an increase in general business activity and just 5.0% anticipating a decline.

### 2026-09-17 20:39:55  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Foreign Stock Investment #Importance 1

Foreign Investment in Canada Falls in July — Foreign investors acquired C$20.7 billion of Canadian securities in July 2026, below the forecast of C$28.64 billion and down sharply from C$41.25 billion in June. Activity was led by strong foreign purchases of federal government bonds, which totaled C$22.7 billion. Since the beginning of the year, foreign investment in federal government bonds has reached an unprecedented C$104.0 billion, compared with C$13.8 billion over the same period in 2025. Consequently, the share of outstanding federal government bonds held by foreign investors rose from 40.2% to 46.5% during the first seven months of 2026. Meanwhile, a C$11.9 billion foreign divestment in money market instruments moderated overall investment, primarily involving private corporate paper (-C$7.6 billion) and federal government paper (-C$3.2 billion). Foreign investors also acquired C$7.2 billion of Canadian shares, the largest monthly investment of 2026.

US Initial Jobless Claims Approach 60-Year Low — The number of people claiming unemployment benefits fell by 10,000 to 196,000 in the second week of September, the least since the 60-year low of 189,000 from July, and well under market expectations of 208,000. Continuing claims, a gauge of outstanding unemployment in the US, fell by 39,000 to 1,730,000 in the first week, the lowest since January of 2024. The data extended the period of resilience in the US labor market as the Federal Reserve cites full employment. Meanwhile, initial claims filed by federal employees, which have added spotlight on the administration's signal to shrink the of public sector, rose by 10 to 398, well below historical levels.

### 2026-09-17 20:36:46  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Housing Starts #Importance 1

US Housing Starts Unexpectedly Fall — Housing starts in the US decreased 2.6% month-over-month to a seasonally adjusted annualized rate of 1.275 million units in August 2026, the second consecutive decline and the lowest level since October last year, amid rising mortgage rates and constrained affordability. It compares with 1.309 million in July and forecasts of 1.31 million. Starts in the multi-family segment slumped 22.5% to 344 thousand while starts for single unit houses were up 7.6% to 918 thousand. Regionally, starts declined in the Northeast (-44.5% to 96 thousand), Midwest (-12% to 198 thousand) and the South (-1.3% to 658 thousand) but rose in the West (32.4% to 323 thousand).

### 2026-09-17 20:22:52  #公司 #市場 #焦點 #國際

【中東供應擾動有望緩解，國際油價延續下跌】有跡象顯示中東供應中斷情況或將緩解，沙特正尋求恢復一條重要管道的部分運力，油價因此繼續走低。布倫特原油週三下跌2.7%，現交投於每桶102美元附近，西德克薩斯中質原油價格跌破100美元。上週沙特東西管道遭無人機襲擊後關停，目前該國計劃在數日內恢復該管道約一半的輸送能力。與此同時，沙特已向亞洲煉油商出售更多原油，可在霍爾木茲海峽外的地點提貨。風險依舊存在：美伊衝突衝擊中東能源流通，俄烏衝突仍在持續。交易員也密切關注葉門胡塞武裝動向，該武裝正向紅海南端關鍵的曼德海峽推進，並持續襲擊沙特石油設施與航運資產。受停火相關報道影響，週四油價進一步下挫。哥本哈根全球風險管理公司首席分析師阿恩·羅曼·拉斯穆森表示：“東西管道部分恢復運營，加上沙特原油透過霍爾木茲海峽開展船對船轉運，短期內對原油構成利空。但只要胡塞衝突持續，市場就難言放鬆。”

【機構：顯示行業告別規模擴張 超大尺寸與OLED成競爭主線】9月16日至17日，2026Omdia中國顯示產業研討會在上海舉辦。大會圍繞大尺寸顯示市場發展進行分析，認為行業總量增長有限，但產品結構向大尺寸快速遷移。同時，國內面板企業主導地位進一步鞏固，高世代OLED產能建設正開啟新競爭週期。Omdia資深首席分析師吳榮兵表示，在OLED賽道，過去韓國廠商佔據高世代OLED接近98%市場份額，隨著京東方B16、華星T8、維信諾V5產線逐步爬坡釋放產能，預計2030年至2031年，國內高世代OLED產能面積佔比有望達到30%，多技術路線並行，本土供應鏈迎來發展機遇。從需求端看，Omdia資料顯示，2026年全球電視面板全年出貨預計約2.4億片，總量小幅回落，但內部尺寸結構發生顯著變化。受儲存、晶片等元器件成本上漲影響，需求向55英寸及以上產品轉移。未來兩到三年，大尺寸面板或將出現旺季搶貨現象。（上證報）

【農業農村部、市場監管總局、國家智慧財產權局聯合實施農業品牌保護專項行動】近日，農業農村部、市場監管總局和國家智慧財產權局聯合印發《農業品牌保護專項行動實施方案（2026—2027年）》（以下簡稱《方案》），提出帶動一批特色品牌、壯大一批品牌主體，集中力量查辦一批侵權違法案件、震懾一批侵權行為，完善部門協同保護機制，健全覆蓋農業品牌培育、管理、使用、推廣的全鏈條保護體系，增強消費信心、提升品牌價值。 本次專項行動圍繞四大重點任務展開。在品牌農產品質量安全監管執法方面，全面落實承諾達標合格證制度，健全問題通報和協查機制，強化產地準出分類監管。在涉農商標、專利領域違法行為方面，嚴厲查處涉農商標侵權、地理標誌侵權、假冒專利等違法行為，強化對農產品網路銷售、直播帶貨中侵權假冒行為的查處力度，嚴厲打擊惡意搶注或侵犯老字號商標、馳名商標的行為。在打擊不正當競爭行為方面，要求重點查處擅自使用知名區域公用品牌、地理標誌品牌等特有的和近似的名稱、包裝、裝潢等混淆行為，重點查處經營者對其商品效能、功能、質量、銷售狀況、使用者評價、曾獲榮譽等作虛假或者引人誤解的商業宣傳，以及透過商業詆譭等方式損害競爭對手的行為。在涉農智慧財產權行政保護方面，支援農業品牌主體透過商標、專利、地理標誌、植物新品種等多維度智慧財產權佈局，強化品牌權益保護。推動智慧財產權公共服務機構聚焦重點農業產業，提供維權諮詢、侵權判定、糾紛調解等專業化公益服務，提升農業品牌主體智慧財產權管理能力。（農業農村部）

Oil and fuel prices trimmed their recent surge after Saudi Arabia signaled progress in restoring capacity through its East-West pipeline, dampening part of the recent surge in wholesale energy inflation. Chip producers led the gains, extending the rebound after concerns over AI safety raised doubts over AI capital expenditure earlier this week. Intel, AMD, Marvell, Seagate, and Lam Research added 3%. 【國際研究機構：尼泊爾泥石流災害與氣候變化密切相關】國際研究機構“世界天氣歸因聯盟”日前釋出分析報告說，氣候變化是8月底尼泊爾邊境地區泥石流災害的一個重要驅動因素。這份題為《喜馬拉雅地區快速變暖加劇連鎖地質災害，超出適應極限》的報告說，這場泥石流災害由尼泊爾境內的藍塘裡壤峰發生冰岩崩引發，水、冰、岩石與沉積物等形成泥石流，席捲河道沿岸，沖毀民居、道路、橋樑等，造成重大災害。報告強調，這場災害並非由單一極端天氣事件引發，而是一場由氣候變暖、冰川退縮、凍土融化及地質不穩定性共同促成的“複合事件”。（新華社）

【河北證監局：上市公司應嚴格依規披露政府補助資訊】9月17日，河北證監局披露了最新一期的《河北上市公司監管通訊》，在監管要求欄目中，要求上市公司應嚴格依規披露政府補助資訊。近期，部分上市公司披露政府補助資訊不合規，部分上市公司將不符合“無償性”原則的政府款項錯誤歸類為政府補助，引發一定關注。各上市公司應按照《企業會計準則第16 號--政府補助》規定確定政府補助範圍，並嚴格按《公開發行證券的公司資訊披露編報規則第15號--財務報告的一般規定》（證監會公告〔2026〕64號）、《公開發行證券的公司資訊披露解釋性公告第1號--非經常性損益》（證監會公告〔2026〕65號）要求在定期報告中披露政府補助資訊，對於單筆重大的政府補助，按照交易所公告格式披露臨時報告。符合資訊披露豁免、暫緩條件的，依規實施資訊披露豁免、暫緩。不得過度披露政府補助資訊博取市場關注，不得虛假披露政府補助資訊騙取市場信任。

### 2026-09-17 19:36:14  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Stock Market #Importance 1

UK Stocks Inch Up After BoE Hold — The FTSE 100 gained ground and rose 0.5% to the 10,750 mark on Thursday after the Bank of England maintained its rate unchanged, as expected by the City. Three members of the Bank's Monetary Policy Council opted for hikes, also aligned with expectations, as policymakers heeded to the impact of higher energy prices on underlying inflation. In turn, equity liquidity was supported by a softer-than-expected unwinding of the BoE balance sheet, lending support to stocks and Gilts. Financial companies hence led the rise in the session, with HSBC and Standard Chartered gaining over 1% each, while 3i added nearly 2%. Meanwhile, SSE rose over 2% as natural gas costs fell for a second session in wholesale markets. Lastly, Next rose 1% after citing a modest improvement in sales expectations and additional cost savings, particularly in warehousing.

### 2026-09-17 19:36:13  #Trading Economics #Economy #Serbia #Current Account #Importance 1

Serbia Current Account Deficit Doubles in July — Serbia’s current account deficit widened sharply to $540 million in July 2026, doubling from a $265 million deficit in the same month a year earlier. The services account swung to a deficit of $80 million from a surplus of $99 million, while the primary income deficit widened to $635 million from $400 million. In contrast, the goods deficit narrowed to $541 million from $607 million, while the secondary income surplus widened to $715 million from $644 million. Over the first seven months of 2026, Serbia posted a current account deficit of $2,199 million, narrowing from $2,396 million in the corresponding period of the previous year.

### 2026-09-17 19:25:07  #Trading Economics #Economy #Ukraine #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Ukraine Delivers Back-to-Back Rate Hikes — The National Bank of Ukraine raised its key policy rate by 50bps to 16% on its September 2026 decision, following up on the 50bps hike in its previous meeting, which was the first hike in 18 months. The Board of the central bank noted that underlying inflationary pressures continued to increase due to second-round effects from supply shocks on global energy markets. Inflation in Ukraine rose to 8.1% in August, slightly ahead of expectations by the central bank, as the escalation in the war in the Middle East triggered a sharper than expected jump in fuel inflation. In turn, the labor market remained relatively stable in the period, with robust employment and higher wage growth. The central bank raised the target range for the federal funds rate by 25bps, marking its first rate hike since July 2023, and signalled at least one further increase in borrowing costs this year. While the move had been fully priced in ahead of the decision, investors had been concerned that a surprise decision to hold rates could trigger a surge in market volatility. Moldova Central Bank Raises Policy Rate to 9% — The National Bank of Moldova raised its benchmark interest rate by 250 basis points to 9% in September 2026, lifting borrowing costs to their highest level since 2023, citing economic uncertainty and volatility in energy markets. Headline inflation accelerated to 6.96% in August, remaining near the upper limit of the central bank’s 5 ± 1.5% target range, reflecting the impact of higher international oil prices on inflationary pressures. However, inflation was lower than expected due to the delayed adjustment of natural gas tariffs. Meanwhile, economic activity accelerated to 0.9% in the second quarter, supported by industry, agriculture, financial and insurance activities, and trade. Looking ahead, inflation is expected to continue rising relatively quickly through the end of 2026, although the outlook will depend on energy and food prices, external financing, the fiscal impulse, and the implementation of the new fiscal policy for 2027. After 2026, policymakers expect disinflation.

Albania Trade Deficit Hits Largest Since 2021 — Albania’s trade deficit widened to ALL 55.0 billion in August 2026 from ALL 46.9 billion in the same month a year earlier, marking the largest deficit since December 2021. Imports jumped 12.6% year-on-year to ALL 75.6 billion, driven largely by higher purchases of minerals, fuels, and electricity (+23.2%), machinery, equipment, and spare parts (+19.8%), and construction materials and metals (+16.5%). Among key trading partners, imports increased from the US (+320.3%), Spain (+27.4%), Turkey (21.9%), and China (+16.9%). Meanwhile, exports rose 1.6% to ALL 20.5 billion, lifted by increased shipments of leather and leather products (+27.3%), wood and articles of paper (+12.0%), and food, beverages, and tobacco (+7.9%). Exports increased the most to Switzerland (+101.9%), the US (+71.6%), China (+49.5%), and Germany (+14.9%). During the January–August period, the country’s trade gap totalled ALL 364.1 billion, up from ALL 348.9 billion in the corresponding month last year.

### 2026-09-17 18:04:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Mongolia #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Mongolia Holds Key Policy Rate at 12.5% — The Bank of Mongolia left its benchmark interest rate at 12.5% at its September 2026 meeting, after raising it by 50 basis points in August. The decision came as annual inflation eased to 12.5% in August from the previous month, while core inflation edged down to 6.8%. Inflation is expected to gradually decline from the second quarter of next year and return to the upper end of the target range by year-end. Nevertheless, policymakers warned that higher fuel and global food prices resulting from the Middle East conflict, together with increased government spending and wages, could reignite inflationary pressures. In particular, the outlook remains vulnerable to fiscal developments, as changes to this year’s budget and the approval of next year’s budget could materially affect the inflation trajectory. Meanwhile, economic growth is expected to reach 7.7% in the first half of 2026, supported mainly by the mining and transport sectors, while other sectors remain relatively weak.

【張升民會見印度尼西亞國防部長】中央軍委副主席張升民17日在北京會見出席第十三屆北京香山論壇的印度尼西亞國防部長沙夫裡。 張升民說，中國和印尼是隔海相望的好鄰居、命運與共的好夥伴。在習近平主席和普拉博沃總統的戰略擘畫下，兩國不斷深化政治、經濟、人文、海上、安全“五大支柱”合作，中印尼全面戰略伙伴關係持續走深走實。雙方緊密團結合作，意義遠超雙邊範疇，應當在國際事務中發揮積極、穩定、向善作用，推動國際秩序朝著更加公正合理的方向發展。安全合作是兩國高度戰略互信的重要體現，兩軍要堅決落實兩國元首重要共識，加強戰略溝通，密切各層級交流互動，拓展各領域務實合作，全面提升兩軍關係，助力中印尼命運共同體建設，為維護世界和平和地區穩定作出更大貢獻。 沙夫裡高度讚賞北京香山論壇對加強防務交流合作、提升全球安全治理髮揮的重要作用。他說，國際和地區的和平穩定與發展繁榮離不開中國，印尼方堅定奉行一箇中國政策，願與中方以元首共識為引領，保持兩國兩軍關係發展良好勢頭，推動雙方務實交流合作取得更多豐碩成果，更好造福兩國和兩國人民。（央視軍事）

### 2026-09-17 17:42:38  #宏觀 #市場

【兩部門規劃搶抓AI機遇，創業板人工智慧ETF華寶（159363）逆勢上漲，流動性優勢顯著】9月17日，創業板人工智慧ETF華寶（159363）逆勢上漲，盤中最高漲1.8%，截至收盤漲幅0.41%，成交額8.46億元，流動性顯著領先同類產品。資金流向方面，昨日獲得8493萬元淨申購。訊息面，近期工信部、國家發改委聯合印發電子資訊製造業發展“十五五”規劃，搶抓人工智慧變革機遇是本次規劃的鮮明主線，提出突破超大規模智算設施架構設計，支援數十萬卡級人工智慧叢集，打造基於計算的人工智慧軟硬體全棧生態。 天風證券指出，堅定看好算力產業鏈核心標的的投資機會，AI帶動算力供應鏈核心標的有望實現較好的業務增長，重視產業鏈核心廠商。國海證券表示，當前HBM供給緊張，但從中期看，隨著國產HBM供應逐步落地，這一供需缺口有望得到緩解。與此同時，AI算力需求景氣度仍在持續走高，行業需求端具備較強支撐。資料顯示，創業板人工智慧ETF華寶（159363）跟蹤指數的一半權重集中在光模組CPO板塊，另一半權重覆蓋國產算力、AI軟體應用領域，形成“硬體+應用”的均衡佈局。全方位把握AI主題機會，關注全市場首隻創業板人工智慧ETF華寶（159363）及場外聯接 （023407.OF，023408.OF）。

【監管再提金融產品網路營銷管理辦法六點要求】財聯社記者獲悉，上海證監局近日向轄區基金管理人、資產管理機構、基金銷售機構下發關於做好《金融產品網路營銷管理辦法》落地實施的通知。《金融產品網路營銷管理辦法》已於2026年4月21日公佈，將於9月30日起正式實施。通知對第三方網際網路平臺合作、自營App建設、轉接渠道以及資訊報送等進一步提出要求。一是不得與第三方網際網路平臺以外的其他組織或者個人，包括“網路大V”，合作開展金融產品網路營銷。二是與第三方網際網路平臺合作應保持業務獨立、技術安全和資料保密，不得允許平臺介入投資者招攬、開戶、交易指令處理、清算交收、基金銷售等業務環節；平臺收費不得與開戶量、交易量、手續費、資金量等業務指標掛鉤或變相掛鉤。三是嚴格落實事前評估機制，重點關注第三方網際網路平臺是否介入證券基金業務環節、是否混淆服務提供主體、是否採取有效措施保障投資者資料安全，以及收費是否合理、質價是否相符等。四是加強自主運營App建設，引導投資者優先使用自身運營管理的App辦理業務，並保障充足、穩定的資訊科技投入，確保第三方平臺發生技術故障等突發事件時仍能持續提供服務。五是不得與未完成資訊科技系統備案的第三方網際網路平臺開展涉及基金申購贖回轉接渠道的合作，存量轉接渠道合作須在《辦法》正式實施1年內完成整改。六是每季度結束後10個工作日內，透過機構監管系統向上海證監局報送與第三方網際網路平臺合作開展的轉接渠道網路營銷有關情況。 (財聯社)

Swiss Bond Yield Eases Further — Switzerland’s 10-year government bond yield fell below 0.58% after reaching its highest level in well over a year, as the Swiss economy remained resilient despite elevated global oil prices due to the conflict in the Middle East and economic uncertainty amid ongoing trade tensions with the US. The State Secretariat for Economic Affairs raised its forecast for economic growth to 1.7% in 2026, up from its June projection of 0.9%, in line with that of the OECD, which also raised its growth forecast to 2% following strong economic performance in the second quarter, when growth accelerated to 1.5%, its highest level in five years. Safe-haven demand also provided support. On the monetary policy front, a Swiss Bankers Association survey showed that all bankers expect the SNB to keep its policy rate at 0% by year-end, as the impact of higher energy prices remains limited. Markets anticipate the first rate hike in June 2027, while most economists expect the first hike in early 2028.

【China maintains independent monetary policy; FX market remains stable】Since August global markets have been choppy but the renminbi has traded broadly stable-to-firmer with two-way swings and China’s FX market has remained orderly, with cross-border flows showing net inflows. Wang Yifeng, deputy director at Everbright Securities’ research institute, said the Fed’s recent hike widened the US–China yield inversion but had been largely priced in and was accompanied by a noticeable rise in US Treasury yields. He described the Fed move as preemptive, aimed at bolstering market confidence amid sticky inflation. Domestic export prospects remain reasonably certain and consumer inflation in China is mild, so the effect of US tightening on cross-border flows is judged manageable. China’s monetary policy remains accommodative with ample liquidity to support real-economy financing, and the renminbi has shown resilience—rate moves in major economies are judged to have limited direct impact on the currency.

【我國貨幣政策堅持以我為主 外匯市場繼續保持穩健執行】美聯儲加息對我國宏觀經濟和貨幣政策走向是否有影響？8月以來，國際金融市場延續震盪走勢，人民幣對美元呈現穩中偏強、雙向波動趨勢，我國外匯市場保持穩健執行，跨境資金保持淨流入。光大證券研究所副所長王一峰表示，本次美聯儲加息後，雖然中美利差倒掛幅度有所擴大，考慮到市場前期對此預期相對充分，近期美債利率已明顯抬高。本次加息屬於預防性加息，反映了美聯儲希望在通脹高位橫盤的背景下，重建市場信任。業內人士表示，我國出口形勢確定性較高，國內通脹水平溫和低位執行，美聯儲加息對跨境資金流動影響可控。我國貨幣政策維持流動性寬鬆，有力支援了實體經濟融資需求。人民幣匯率韌性十足，海外主要經濟體加息與否對人民幣匯率直接影響較小。（央視財經）

【Xi'an housing provident fund widens withdrawals; renovation cap 200,000 yuan】Xi'an Housing Provident Fund Management Center issued a notice widening withdrawal scope, effective Sept. 20, 2026. Renovation: for owner-occupied homes in Xi'an bought within the past 1 year (inclusive) or held as owner-occupied for at least 15 years, owners and their spouses may make a one-time withdrawal of their provident fund balance to pay renovation costs for one residence; amount is calculated at 1,500 yuan per sq. metre (certificate area), capped at 200,000 yuan. Property fees: owners and spouses may apply once every 12 months to withdraw funds to pay annual property-management fees for one residence; caps are 3,000 yuan/year for ≤90 sq. m and 4,000 yuan/year for >90 sq. m. Dilapidated-house renewal: owners in Xi'an who meet renewal criteria, plus their spouses, parents and children, may withdraw funds to cover renewal costs up to actual renovation expenses.

### 2026-09-17 17:27:15  #其他

【西安住房公積金政策調整 支援裝修自住住房提取上限20萬元】9月17日，西安住房公積金管理中心釋出關於拓寬住房公積金提取範圍的通知，內容如下：
一、支援裝修自住住房提取
在西安市行政區域1年（含）內新購自住住房或擁有自住住房滿15年的，購房人（產權人）及配偶可一次性提取公積金賬戶餘額用於支付1套住房裝修費用。提取額度按不動產權證面積計算，每平方米1500元，上限20萬元。
二、支援自住住房物業費提取
在西安市行政區域擁有自住住房的，產權人及配偶每滿12個月可申請提取一次住房公積金用於支付當年1套住房物業服務費。提取額度標準為建築面積90㎡（含）以下，每年不超過3000元；90㎡以上，每年不超過4000元。
三、支援危舊房更新提取
在西安市行政區域內擁有自住住房，符合危舊房更新條件的，產權人及其配偶、父母、子女可以提取公積金用於危舊房更新。提取總額不超過實際改造費用。
本通知自2026年9月20日起施行。

### 2026-09-17 17:27:00  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malta #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Malta Inflation Edges Down in August — Malta’s annual inflation rate edged down to 2.0% in August 2026 from 2.1% in the previous month. Prices increased at a softer pace in food and non-alcoholic beverages (1.6% vs 1.7% in July), furnishings and household equipment (1.1% vs 2.9%), restaurants and hotels (2.9% vs 3.3%), and insurance and financial services (0.7% vs 1.0%), while they remained unchanged for alcoholic beverages and tobacco (1.8%), health (2.8%), recreation and culture (3.2%), education (3.2%), and miscellaneous goods and services (3.7%). Costs in information and communication (-3.4% vs -1.6%) also declined further. Meanwhile, prices rebounded in clothing and footwear (1.6% vs -3.6%), while they continued to increase in transportation (2.3% vs 1.9%) and housing and utilities (3.1% vs 3.0%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.6% in August, following a 0.5% increase in the previous month.

### 2026-09-17 17:17:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Taiwan #Interest Rate #Importance 1

Taiwan Leaves Key Rate Unchanged — The Central Bank of the Republic of China, Taiwan’s monetary authority, kept its benchmark interest rate unchanged at 2% at its September 2026 meeting, holding borrowing costs at their highest level since 2008. Headline inflation remained above the central bank’s 2% target for a fourth consecutive month, although it eased to 2.04% in August, reflecting the impact of higher international oil prices. Meanwhile, economic growth remained resilient, with the economy recording its fastest first-half growth since 1976, fueled by the AI boom and strong export growth, particularly in semiconductors. Forecasts for economic growth were raised to 9.09% for the second half of 2026 and 11.48% for the full year, as demand for emerging technologies is expected to remain strong. The inflation forecast was also raised to 2.03%, before easing to 1.83% in 2027 as the surge in oil prices subsides.

### 2026-09-17 17:13:47  #公司

【華為楊超斌：以靈衢互聯為核心，打造叢集與超節點協同的新計算架構】在華為全聯接大會2026期間，華為常務董事、ICT BG CEO楊超斌發表主題演講，圍繞計算架構創新，系統闡釋了靈衢互聯架構，並宣佈推出基於靈衢互聯架構的系列化算力底座，使能客戶、夥伴與開發者應用創新。在傳統計算架構下，叢集規模擴大時，資源利用率反而下降，十萬卡叢集的實際模型算力利用率僅約20%，大量算力處於等待通訊完成的狀態。為此，華為以靈衢互聯為核心，打造叢集與超節點協同的新計算架構，以支援Agentic AI時代爆發式增長的計算需求。基於靈衢的計算系統具備四大特徵：一、協議歸一，記憶體語義。二、多樣算力，異構協同。三、分級儲存，全域性池化。四、光電互聯，靈活組網。（新浪科技）

### 2026-09-17 17:13:42  #Trading Economics #Economy #Euro Area #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Eurozone Inflation Remains Elevated as Energy Costs Soar — Eurozone annual inflation accelerated to 3.2% in August 2026, matching May’s two-and-a-half-year high but coming in slightly below the preliminary estimate of 3.3%. The rate remained well above the ECB’s 2% target, driven largely by a surge in energy prices amid continued fighting in the Middle East. Energy inflation jumped to 14.3%, its highest since January 2023, while inflation for unprocessed food and non-energy industrial goods also accelerated. Services inflation, however, eased to a four-month low of 3.0%, while core inflation, excluding energy and food, edged down to 2.4% from 2.5%. Among the euro area’s largest economies, inflation accelerated in Germany to 2.9% from 2.8%, France to 2.6% from 2.4%, Spain to 4.6% from 3.9%, and Italy to 3.2% from 2.9%. The Netherlands was an exception, with inflation easing to 2.8% from 3.0%.
